    1503             : namespace versioning_internal {

    1505             : /* Specific code required to properly handle older blend-files (pre-2.83), where some node data
    1506             :  * (like the sockets default values) were written as raw bytes buffer, without any DNA type
    1507             :  * information. */
    1508             : 
    1509             : /* Node socket default values were historically written and read as raw bytes buffers, without any
    1510             :  * DNA typing information.
    1511             :  *
    1512             :  * The writing code was fixed in commit `50d5050e9c`, which is included in the 2.83 release.
    1513             :  * However the matching reading code was only fixed in the 4.5 release.
    1514             :  *
    1515             :  * So currently, reading code assumes that any blend-file >= 3.0 has correct DNA info for these
    1516             :  * default values, and it keeps previous 'raw buffer' reading code for older ones.
    1517             :  *
    1518             :  * This means that special care must be taken when the various DNA types used for these default
    1519             :  * values are modified, as a 'manual' version of DNA internal versioning must be performed on data
    1520             :  * from older blend-files (see also #direct_link_node_socket_default_value).
    1521             :  */
    1522             : constexpr int MIN_BLENDFILE_VERSION_FOR_MODERN_NODE_SOCKET_DEFAULT_VALUE_READING = 300;
    1523             : 
    1524             : /* The `_404` structs below are copies of DNA structs as they were in Blender 4.4 and before. Their
    1525             :  * data layout should never have to be modified in any way, as it matches the expected data layout
    1526             :  * in the raw bytes buffers read from older blend-files.
    1527             :  *
    1528             :  * NOTE: There is _no_ need to protect DNA structs definition in any way to ensure forward
    1529             :  * compatibility, for the following reasons:
    1530             :  *   - The DNA struct info _is_ properly written in blend-files since 2.83.
    1531             :  *   - When there is DNA info for a #BHead in the blend-file, even if that #BHead is ultimately
    1532             :  *     'read'/used as raw bytes buffer through a call to `BLO_read_raw_address`, the actual
    1533             :  *     reading of that #BHead from the blend-file will have already gone through the lower-level
    1534             :  *     'DNA versioning' process, which means that DNA struct changes (like adding new properties,
    1535             :  *     increasing an array size, etc.) will be handled properly.
    1536             :  *   - Blender versions prior to 3.6 will not be able to load any 4.0+ blend-files without
    1537             :  *     immediate crash, so trying to preserve forward compatibility for versions older than
    1538             :  *     2.83 would be totally pointless.
    1539             :  */
    1540             :
